evidence: unknown. Targets: CultureMech:007187.

The abstract's hypothetical animal-like precursor and plant-kingdom evolutionary language do not identify experimental taxa; those descriptive phrases remain unresolved without invented binomials. No individually named representative or exact local Vogel N formulation is established by the abstract. Historical 'amoung' title spelling is retained as source metadata. The existing JSTOR bibliography entry is enriched, not duplicated; its original reference URL and all other entries remain intact. The pinned MediaDB source-to-owner join establishes a historical bibliographic lead only, not exact recipe equivalence. Formulation identity, study-specific differences and variant destination remain unassessed in this non-operational citation task. No cultivation conditions, optimization, virulence, growth associations or ingredient amounts are extracted or curated. 

### Scientific names, ranks and study roles

evidence: supported. Targets: CultureMech:007187.

fungi: Fungi, NCBITaxon:4751, actual rank kingdom; broad_group_context_not_all_members; title and abstract group, not a growth association. basidiomycetes: Basidiomycota, NCBITaxon:5204, actual rank phylum; verified_common_name_actual_rank_phylum; abstract group, not individually identified representatives. ascomycetes: Fungi, NCBITaxon:4751, actual rank kingdom; parent_rank_only; historical collective label; exact historical circumscription not verified; Fungi is a broad parent, not its identity. phycomycetes: Eukaryota, NCBITaxon:2759, actual rank domain; parent_rank_only; historical collective label unresolved as a modern taxon; Eukaryota is only a broad parent context. euglenids: Euglenida, NCBITaxon:3035, actual rank clade; verified_common_name_actual_rank_clade; comparative abstract mention, not a medium association. Neurospora crassa OR74A: Neurospora crassa, NCBITaxon:5141, actual rank species; parent_rank_only_secondary_import_lead; MediaDB organism lead only; species verified, exact strain and primary-paper occurrence not established. No new growth assertions.

## Findings

### merge-gates: Repository-wide merge verification and freshness gates failed

major / open / confirmed; issue key: bibliographic-taxonomy-citations-merge-gates.

Both current derivation gates failed. These are global diagnostics, not attribution to the current bibliography edits. One native reference check also failed on a JSTOR challenge-page title and is tracked separately; derived outputs were not published.

### metadata-84: PubMed and publisher bibliographic titles differ

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: bibliographic-taxonomy-citations-metadata-84.

PubMed title contains 'balanced grown'; publisher title contains 'Balanced Growth'. Notes retain both authorities rather than silently claiming a correction.

### reference-access-98: Native JSTOR title check received a Client Challenge page

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: bibliographic-taxonomy-citations-reference-access-98.

One post-edit reference check failed because it compared a challenge-page title with the verified article title. Archived publisher, Crossref and JSTOR landing-page identity evidence supports the retained title, but does not make the failed native check pass.

### non-operational-boundary: Bibliography and taxonomy do not establish recipe or growth claims

informational / open / confirmed; issue key: bibliographic-taxonomy-citations-non-operational-boundary.

Seventeen titles and one abstract group inventory were assessed. Archived abstracts are not experimental approval. Recipe differences, variants, cultivation conditions and growth assertions remain outside this task.

## Recommended Actions And Acceptance Checks

### resolve-merge-gates

Repository-wide merge verification and freshness gates failed

- Resolve repository derivation defects in a separately scoped change and rerun both gates; no automatic publication.

### resolve-metadata-84

PubMed and publisher bibliographic titles differ

- Preserve both source forms until authoritative bibliographic reconciliation is documented.

### resolve-reference-access-98

Native JSTOR title check received a Client Challenge page

- Restore normal bibliographic access or separately improve challenge-page handling with tests, then rerun the native check. Do not fabricate cache data or replace the article title with the challenge title.

### resolve-non-operational-boundary

Bibliography and taxonomy do not establish recipe or growth claims

- Keep bibliographic identity distinct from experimental evidence; preserve existing recipe fields without certifying them.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Partial self-review, not independent review or scientific record approval.
- Seventeen title-level inventories and Vogel abstract groups only, not exhaustive abstract or whole-paper organism review.
- NCBI ranks and parent-only mappings remain qualified; secondary import names are not primary title evidence.
- Existing JSTOR identifier is retained and enriched, not counted as a newly added citation.
- One native reference-title check remains failed; alternate identity evidence is not a waiver or passing result.
- Merge diagnostics retain actual failures; equal counts cannot establish row-level equivalence.
- No generated or GitHub publication. Source access gaps and broader discovery leave the citation goal incomplete.

  area: evidence

  outcome: unknown
  target_ids:

  evidence_ids:

  - claims
  - comparison
  summary: 'Review is limited to publication identity, title-level organism mentions
    and taxonomy. Abstract content is archived but not used here to assert experimental
    findings. This is not an exhaustive abstract or whole-paper organism inventory.
    The pinned MediaDB source-to-owner join establishes a historical bibliographic
    lead only, not exact recipe equivalence. Formulation identity, study-specific
    differences and variant destination remain unassessed in this non-operational
    citation task. No cultivation conditions, optimization, virulence, growth associations
    or ingredient amounts are extracted or curated. '

- assessment_id: scope-007187
  area: evidence
  topic: Formulation and experimental claims unassessed
  outcome: unknown
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007187
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-98
  - jstor-98
  - claims
  - comparison
  summary: 'The abstract''s hypothetical animal-like precursor and plant-kingdom evolutionary
    language do not identify experimental taxa; those descriptive phrases remain unresolved
    without invented binomials. No individually named representative or exact local
    Vogel N formulation is established by the abstract. Historical ''amoung'' title
    spelling is retained as source metadata. The existing JSTOR bibliography entry
    is enriched, not duplicated; its original reference URL and all other entries
    remain intact. The pinned MediaDB source-to-owner join establishes a historical
    bibliographic lead only, not exact recipe equivalence. Formulation identity, study-specific
    differences and variant destination remain unassessed in this non-operational
    citation task. No cultivation conditions, optimization, virulence, growth associations
    or ingredient amounts are extracted or curated. '
- assessment_id: organism-007187
  area: evidence
  topic: Scientific names, ranks and study roles
  outcome: supported
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007187
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-98
  - jstor-98
  - taxonomy-0
  - taxonomy-1
  summary: 'fungi: Fungi, NCBITaxon:4751, actual rank kingdom; broad_group_context_not_all_members;
    title and abstract group, not a growth association. basidiomycetes: Basidiomycota,
    NCBITaxon:5204, actual rank phylum; verified_common_name_actual_rank_phylum; abstract
    group, not individually identified representatives. ascomycetes: Fungi, NCBITaxon:4751,
    actual rank kingdom; parent_rank_only; historical collective label; exact historical
    circumscription not verified; Fungi is a broad parent, not its identity. phycomycetes:
    Eukaryota, NCBITaxon:2759, actual rank domain; parent_rank_only; historical collective
    label unresolved as a modern taxon; Eukaryota is only a broad parent context.
    euglenids: Euglenida, NCBITaxon:3035, actual rank clade; verified_common_name_actual_rank_clade;
    comparative abstract mention, not a medium association. Neurospora crassa OR74A:
    Neurospora crassa, NCBITaxon:5141, actual rank species; parent_rank_only_secondary_import_lead;
    MediaDB organism lead only; species verified, exact strain and primary-paper occurrence
    not established. No new growth assertions.'

  - Keep bibliographic identity distinct from experimental evidence; preserve existing
    recipe fields without certifying them.
limitations:
- Partial self-review, not independent review or scientific record approval.
- Seventeen title-level inventories and Vogel abstract groups only, not exhaustive
  abstract or whole-paper organism review.
- NCBI ranks and parent-only mappings remain qualified; secondary import names are
  not primary title evidence.
- Existing JSTOR identifier is retained and enriched, not counted as a newly added
  citation.
- One native reference-title check remains failed; alternate identity evidence is
  not a waiver or passing result.
- Merge diagnostics retain actual failures; equal counts cannot establish row-level
  equivalence.
- No generated or GitHub publication. Source access gaps and broader discovery leave
  the citation goal incomplete.
